# Blockstorage

This guide describes the public Go APIs and their contracts against the pinned Gophercloud and Python openstacksdk sources. Support decisions apply to each operation below.

See the [support ledger](../docs/sdk-support-ledger.md) for status definitions and shared limitations.

## Operation contracts

### `python:cloud/_block_storage/BlockStorageCloudMixin/attach_volume`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk.Connection.AttachVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.AttachVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithAttachVolumeOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithAttachVolumeDevice`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithAttachVolumeWait`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithAttachVolumeWaitPolicy`

- AttachVolume defaults to omitted device, enabled waiting, no SDK wait deadline, two-second polling and exact case-insensitive error failure state. Explicit IDs perform fresh Cinder GET200, Nova POST200 with volumeAttachment.volumeId and optional nonempty device, then fresh fixed-ID Cinder waiting GET200; Wait(false) keeps the precondition GET and omits polling.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsDefaultSequenceAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsExplicitNoWaitAndLiteralDevice`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_options_test.go:TestAttachVolumeOptionsConcreteDefaultsAndReplacementOrder`.
- Nil or canceled contexts, invalid request references, either invalid source, malformed device or wait policy and nil options stop before name resolution and workflow requests. Disabled wait fields still receive complete validation. Ordered callbacks are applied once and pointer/slice values are copied after callbacks; whole and subtree replacement helpers preserve nil versus nonnil empty failures.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsPreflightBeforeCallbacksOrHTTP`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_options_test.go:TestAttachVolumeOptionsFactoriesOwnReusablePointersAndSlices`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_options_test.go:TestAttachVolumeOptionsExternalCallbacksRunOnceAndRetainedConfigIsolated`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_options_test.go:TestAttachVolumeOptionsValidateInactiveWaitPolicyAndStopOnGuardFailure`.
- Server and volume names use their existing ordinary native Resources.ResolveID once in order with exact names, duplicate and missing handling; explicit IDs do not fetch the server. Resolved IDs become fixed routes, and response IDs, Location and mutable source configuration cannot select another logical target.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsNamesResolveOnceBeforeFreshCheck`.
- The fresh Cinder precondition requires canonical id equal to the fixed volume ID, exact available status and an explicit nonnull attachments array. It rejects any association with the target server even if device is omitted or empty, rejects null rows and preserves the accepted Checked evidence without a Nova POST on failure.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsFreshPreconditionForbidsUnsafeMutation`.
- Nova POST uses the actual server os-volume_attachments route and the minimal volumeAttachment envelope rather than Cinder attachments. Canonical volumeId must match the fixed volume ID; present nonnull serverId must match the server. Attachment id, attachment_id and bdm_uuid remain passive response fields and a mismatch preserves actual Created proof before stopping.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsNovaStatusAndCreatedDecodeFailuresAreTerminal`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsDistinctProvidersAndNullableOptionalServer`.
- Owned Cinder observation, nested association and Nova attachment models decode exact canonical keys atomically. Known optional fields distinguish nullable pointers while raw metadata retains omission versus null, unknown fields, exact large numeric literals and literal timestamps; malformed UTF-8, nonobject envelopes, wrong known field types and malformed attachment rows fail without partial typed assignment.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsCanonicalNullableFieldsAndRawExtensions`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsDistinguishAbsentNullAndEmptyAttachments`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsRejectInvalidFieldsAtomically`.
- Checked, Created and LastAccepted retain actual accepted response bytes, cloned headers and status before read, Close, decode or later guard failures. Responses, raw fields, typed models and Ready from separate phases own independent data. A nonnil result alone does not establish a mutation; Created HTTP200 records acknowledgement and only successful waiting populates Ready.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsDefaultSequenceAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsAcceptedBodyFailuresRetainPhaseProofWithoutResend`.
- Waiting begins after the Nova response is decoded and verified. Nil or zero timeout is unlimited; positive timeout is wait-only and caller context bounds all phases. Each poll requires matching canonical Cinder id and known status, checks case-insensitive in-use before exact failures and does not infer error-prefix states. Nonnil empty failure states disable failure matching. Negative timeout fails before lookup or mutation even when waiting is disabled. A positive wait deadline cancels polling HTTP and timer waits through context; this deliberately differs from Python zero/negative and between-fetch timer semantics.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsWaitBudgetStartsAfterCreatedResponse`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsExactWaitStatesTargetFirstAndCallback`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutAndCancellationKeepLastAccepted`.
- Nonterminal waiting responses invoke a synchronous ProgressCallback with zero rather than reading an unmodeled extension progress field. Callback errors and cancellation retain their original inspectable causes and already accepted evidence; target and failure observations do not invoke progress and timer/context waiting remains cancellable.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsExactWaitStatesTargetFirstAndCallback`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutAndCancellationKeepLastAccepted`.
- Rejected HTTP errors preserve native method, URL, accepted-code policy, physical status/body/header and original causes without synthesizing accepted phase wrappers. LastAccepted means the last accepted waiting Cinder response and remains intact when a later rejected request or context error occurs. Later failure never triggers automatic detach, delete or a new logical attach operation.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsCreatedProofSurvivesWaitFailures`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutAndCancellationKeepLastAccepted`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsNovaStatusAndCreatedDecodeFailuresAreTerminal`.
- Both selected clients retain their effective prefixed ResourceBase, validated service type and microversion with entry-snapshotted canonical ordinary source headers and original live provider authentication. Provider/Endpoint/ResourceBase/Type/Microversion changes are terminal before another phase. Native configured reauthentication and retry remain distinct from SDK guards for method, URL, serialized JSON and accepted-response ownership.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsSourceSnapshotsLiveTokenAndConsistentVersions`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsRejectSourceChangesAtOptionsAndAfterMutation`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsNativeReauthenticationBackoffAndOwnedRetry`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsNativeRetryCannotReplaceOwnedRequestOrStatusPolicy`.
- Connection.AttachVolume validates context, receiver and references before selecting cached ComputeV2 and BlockStorageV3 clients, then delegates to the package workflow without holding a Connection mutex through callbacks. Lazy service initialization or microversion discovery can precede package option validation, while options still complete before name/precondition/mutation/poll requests.
  Tests: `connection_attach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionAttachVolumeSharesCachedClientsAndLiveToken`, `connection_attach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionAttachVolumeDiscoveryPrecedesOptionsAndIsCached`, `connection_attach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionAttachVolumeValidatesBeforeClientDiscovery`, `connection_attach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionAttachVolumeServiceInitializationCancellationCause`.
- The workflow owns exact case-insensitive error matching without changing the existing high-level Cinder Collection waiter, which retains its error-prefix failure policy.
  Tests: `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsExactWaitStatesTargetFirstAndCallback`, `blockstorage/blockstorage_test.go:TestCinderFailurePrefixAndMicroversion`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go accepts resource.Ref inputs and performs fresh Cinder checks after optional name resolution. Pinned Python accepts caller-owned mutable server and volume Resource/dict values, reads their supplied available status and attachment records and only rejects a matching server when get_volume_attach_device returns a truthy device. Go deliberately rejects a matching association independent of device and requires explicit canonical identity/status/attachments proof.
- Go waiting always fetches fresh fixed-ID Cinder responses and uses its owned exact-error policy. Python may immediately return a supplied Resource whose cached status already equals in-use; dict inputs are refetched before passing a Resource into the waiter. Both default to enabled wait, two-second polls and an unlimited wait-only SDK timeout, while caller context and error-returning progress callbacks are Go controls.
- Go returns completed accepted phase proof and last accepted waiting evidence alongside read/decode/callback/context/native errors and leaves existing side effects to the caller. Python raises on waiting failure without returning the Nova attachment. Raw evidence is separate from successfully decoded typed results and is not a complete physical-attempt log.
- Go uses strict owned canonical JSON fields, nullable response pointers, raw extension precision, literal timestamps and fixed route identities. Absent Nova id stays nil rather than adopting volumeId as Python alternate identity; serverId reads only actual response data, while selected server identity remains in the workflow result. It does not reproduce Python descriptor coercion or Resource object mutation to choose status or route. Optional Nova response attributes remain passive and no arbitrary attachment attrs are added to this device-only cloud helper.
- Go captures selected source clients and normal headers while using their original live provider authentication. Source configuration changes between phases are terminal; Connection may initialize or discover services before package callback application. Configured native retry/reauthentication remain in place and packet-level exactly-once is not claimed.
- Go intentionally uses nil or numeric-zero Timeout as no SDK wait deadline and rejects negative Timeout before any name lookup or workflow mutation, including when Wait is false. Pinned Python forwards timeout to Resource waiting after the Nova mutation; utils.iterate_timeout treats numeric zero or negative timeout as immediate ResourceTimeout before its first poll unless an already-target Resource short-circuits first. Positive Go wait deadlines cancel HTTP and timers via context, whereas Python checks elapsed time between fetches and does not interrupt an in-flight fetch or sleep with that loop deadline.
- This mapping closes the directly declared cloud attach_volume parameter/control-flow contract using the documented Go-native representation. It does not promote the separately inventoried Compute create_volume_attachment, Cinder get_volume or generic wait_for_status declarations or their inherited Resource/session/cache behavior; their full SDK reviews remain independent.

### `python:cloud/_block_storage/BlockStorageCloudMixin/detach_volume`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk.Connection.DetachVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.DetachVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.PrepareDetachVolumeOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithDetachVolumeOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithDetachVolumeWait`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithDetachVolumeWaitPolicy`

- DetachVolume maps the directly declared cloud helper with fixed Server and Volume references, enabled wait by default, an unlimited SDK wait deadline, two-second polling and exact case-insensitive error failure state. Explicit IDs perform bodyless Nova DELETE accepted only at202/204 followed by an immediate fresh fixed-ID Cinder GET200; no pre-delete server, attachment or Cinder state GET is added.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsNoWaitIDsNeedOnlyNovaAndOpaqueAcknowledgement`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsPreparationOwnsEffectiveDefaultsAndReplacementOrder`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsAvailableBeforeExactFailuresAndStatusOnlyReadiness`.
- Wait(false) with a volume ID supports a Nova-only package call with nil Cinder and returns only the accepted Deleted proof. A server Name resolves through Nova without requiring Cinder; a volume Name still requires Cinder. All selected source, reference, context and concrete option policies are validated before name lookup or deletion, and invalid inactive wait policy is rejected even when waiting is false.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsNoWaitIDsNeedOnlyNovaAndOpaqueAcknowledgement`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsPreflightAndRequiredCinderBeforeNameLookup`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsNamesResolveOnceServerThenVolumeWithoutSwap`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsValidateInactivePolicyAndContextCauses`.
- Whole and wait-subtree factories snapshot reusable pointers and slices, preserve nil versus nonnil empty failures and apply replacement in order. PrepareDetachVolumeOptions applies original external callbacks once, validates the complete effective policy and returns an independently owned DetachVolumeOpts without SDK HTTP or service initialization. Reapplying its concrete result never reruns the original callbacks.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsPreparationOwnsEffectiveDefaultsAndReplacementOrder`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsFactoriesAreReusableOwnedSnapshots`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsCallbacksRunOnceAndCannotRetainPreparedPolicy`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsValidateInactivePolicyAndContextCauses`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeSharesCachedClientsAndLiveToken`.
- Direct package preparation captures the required selected client sources before original option callbacks, retaining live provider authentication and entry headers. Connection validates context, receiver and references, prepares the original options before service discovery, selects cached Nova and only necessary Cinder, then delegates with the concrete prepared policy without a mutex around caller callbacks.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportSnapshotsHeadersLiveDistinctProvidersAndSourceGuards`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeOptionsPrecedeDiscoveryAndAreCached`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeSharesCachedClientsAndLiveToken`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeValidatesBeforeClientDiscovery`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeNoWaitNeedsOnlyNova`.
- Server and volume Name references resolve once in their existing exact native collections, server then volume, with missing/duplicate failures before mutation. Resolved IDs stay fixed, the final Nova route segment is the volume ID and the workflow does not apply Python server-volume argument swapping, attachment-ID substitution, Location retargeting or fixed-ID fallback searches.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsNamesResolveOnceServerThenVolumeWithoutSwap`, `resource/resolve_test.go:TestResolveIDUsesExactNamesAndNeverFetchesExplicitIDs`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`.
- Only actual Nova202/204 responses populate Deleted with opaque original body bytes, cloned headers and actual status. Empty, binary and non-JSON accepted bodies require no JSON decoding. Accepted read, Close, cancellation or post-source-check errors retain partial or complete Deleted proof and original causes without starting waiting or resending the completed phase.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsNoWaitIDsNeedOnlyNovaAndOpaqueAcknowledgement`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportAcceptedReadCloseContextAndSourceFailuresRetainOpaqueProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportSnapshotsHeadersLiveDistinctProvidersAndSourceGuards`.
- Nova404 is a failure rather than an ignored missing attachment. Other rejected status codes preserve native method, URL, expected202204, physical status/body/header and original cause, leave Deleted nil and never poll. The workflow does not call Cinder os-detach, delete the volume, force cleanup or attach again as compensation.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsMissingAndUnexpectedDeleteCodesKeepNativeFailure`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportRejectedIOAndContextDoNotInventAcceptedAcknowledgement`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`.
- Waiting always fetches the selected fixed Cinder volume ID and requires matching canonical nonnull id and status. It tests exact case-insensitive available before configured failures, uses error rather than error-prefix matching, permits absent/null attachments and rejects malformed known fields or null array rows. A multiattach volume remaining in-use is nonterminal even when the target association is absent; Cinder404 or disappearance fails rather than completing the operation.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsAvailableBeforeExactFailuresAndStatusOnlyReadiness`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsAcceptedInvalidPollsAnd404PreserveAcknowledgement`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportSnapshotsHeadersLiveDistinctProvidersAndSourceGuards`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsCanonicalNullableFieldsAndRawExtensions`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsDistinguishAbsentNullAndEmptyAttachments`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsRejectInvalidFieldsAtomically`.
- Nil or zero Timeout is unlimited, positive Timeout begins after accepted DELETE body reading/Close and source validation, and negative Timeout fails before lookup or deletion even with wait disabled. Positive deadline and caller context cancel poll HTTP and timer waits. This implements a functional Go wait policy while pinned Python declares timeout but never forwards it to its post-delete lookup or waiter.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportWaitBudgetStartsAfterDeleteAndZeroRemainsUnlimited`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutCancellationAndLaterRejectionKeepLastAccepted`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportBlockedPollCancellationKeepsPriorAcceptedObservation`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_options_test.go:TestDetachVolumeOptionsValidateInactivePolicyAndContextCauses`.
- Only nonterminal observations invoke ProgressCallback with zero. Its error or custom context cancellation preserves original inspectable causes alongside accepted Deleted and LastAccepted proof; target and failure observations do not invoke it. Nonnil empty FailureStates disable exact failure matching and an available target wins even when also configured as a failure.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsAvailableBeforeExactFailuresAndStatusOnlyReadiness`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutCancellationAndLaterRejectionKeepLastAccepted`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportWaitBudgetStartsAfterDeleteAndZeroRemainsUnlimited`.
- LastAccepted is captured from each actual accepted Cinder response before read, Close, canonical decode or later guard failures; later rejected HTTP/transport errors keep their own native evidence without overwriting it. Ready is independently decoded from successful available proof so model pointers, headers, raw metadata and nested association records do not alias LastAccepted.Volume or earlier phases.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsAcceptedInvalidPollsAnd404PreserveAcknowledgement`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportAcceptedReadCloseContextAndSourceFailuresRetainOpaqueProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutCancellationAndLaterRejectionKeepLastAccepted`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportBlockedPollCancellationKeepsPriorAcceptedObservation`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportLaterFailureKeepsPriorAcceptedProof`.
- Captured effective ResourceBase prefixes, consistent service type/microversion and canonical ordinary headers remain fixed while original live tokens, configured reauthentication, backoff and RetryFunc remain provider-owned. Required source Provider/Endpoint/ResourceBase/Type/Microversion replacement is terminal. SDK fixed-request ownership prevents native callback replacement of logical method, URL, bodyless request or accepted response policy without claiming packet-level exactly-once.
  Tests: `blockstorage/detach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestDetachVolumeContractsDefaultDeleteThenFreshAvailableAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportSnapshotsHeadersLiveDistinctProvidersAndSourceGuards`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportNativeAuthBackoffAndRetryPreserveBodylessFixedDelete`, `blockstorage/detach_volume_transport_test.go:TestDetachVolumeTransportNativeRetryCannotAddBodyOrExpandAcceptedProof`.
- Connection no-wait volume-ID calls initialize only cached Nova, while default wait and volume Name require cached Cinder. Preparation errors and invalid receiver/context/references precede service discovery; client-initialization cancellation retains context.Canceled and a custom cause. Reuse of attachment source/model/wait helpers preserves existing AttachVolume POST200, fresh available/association guards, options, proof ownership and the older Cinder Collection error-prefix waiter.
  Tests: `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeNoWaitNeedsOnlyNova`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeOptionsPrecedeDiscoveryAndAreCached`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeValidatesBeforeClientDiscovery`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeServiceInitializationCancellationCause`, `connection_detach_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDetachVolumeSharesCachedClientsAndLiveToken`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsDefaultSequenceAndIndependentProof`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsFreshPreconditionForbidsUnsafeMutation`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsExactWaitStatesTargetFirstAndCallback`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_contracts_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsWaitTimeoutAndCancellationKeepLastAccepted`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsAcceptedBodyFailuresRetainPhaseProofWithoutResend`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsSourceSnapshotsLiveTokenAndConsistentVersions`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_transport_test.go:TestAttachVolumeContractsNativeRetryCannotReplaceOwnedRequestOrStatusPolicy`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_options_test.go:TestAttachVolumeOptionsConcreteDefaultsAndReplacementOrder`, `blockstorage/attach_volume_models_test.go:TestAttachVolumeModelsRejectInvalidFieldsAtomically`, `blockstorage/blockstorage_test.go:TestCinderFailurePrefixAndMicroversion`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go accepts explicit Server/Volume resource.Ref values and resolves names once. Pinned cloud helper indexes caller Resource/dict values for IDs and passes strings into the Compute proxy; that proxy may find_server and reverse server/volume arguments through a compatibility heuristic. Go never guesses or swaps the caller roles and fixes deletion to the chosen volume ID, not attachment identity.
- Go defaults to the same enabled available wait with two-second polling, exact error failure detection and no SDK wait deadline, but every post-delete observation is a fresh fixed-ID GET with canonical identity/status proof. Pinned cloud helper calls cloud get_volume, which delegates to find_volume and may obtain a Resource that immediately short-circuits on cached available state. Missing post-delete Cinder observations remain failures in both workflows.
- Pinned Python declares timeout but ignores it: it is never passed to get_volume or wait_for_status. Go intentionally honors a positive wait-only Timeout after the Nova acknowledgement has been read and closed, uses nil/zero for unlimited waiting and rejects negative/invalid inactive policies before mutation. Caller context additionally controls every phase; positive context deadlines interrupt polling HTTP and timers rather than only checking elapsed time between Python fetches.
- Python success returns None and an exception exposes no accepted delete or last observed volume through the helper result. Go returns Deleted, LastAccepted and independently owned Ready alongside processing/native/callback/context errors, retains opaque202/204 body bytes without JSON parsing and leaves accepted side effects for caller follow-up. The accepted-phase result is not a complete physical-attempt trace.
- Go provides a concrete owned policy and a local PrepareDetachVolumeOptions seam. Connection applies original option callbacks before service discovery and selects Cinder only for waiting or volume Name; package callers may use only Nova with wait false and a volume ID. Direct package calls capture required sources before original callbacks; both use frozen selected client settings and current authentication from original providers.
- Go retains raw JSON precision, nullable canonical fields and literal timestamps in its reused volume observation models rather than reproducing mutable Python Resource descriptors, alternate identity, dirty state, cache and injected session/adapter objects. ProgressCallback and caller-selectable failure states are explicit Go extensions; this helper does not expose Cinder force/connector detach action controls.
- This go_mapping closes only the directly declared cloud detach_volume parameter/control-flow contract and its documented Go adaptation. Separately inventoried Compute delete_volume_attachment, cloud get_volume, Cinder find/wait and inherited Resource declarations retain independent reviews; native leaves and other volume workflows are not promoted.

### `python:cloud/_block_storage/BlockStorageCloudMixin/create_volume`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk.Connection.CreateVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.CreateVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.PrepareCreateVolumeOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeAttributes`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeWait`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeBootable`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeWaitPolicy`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeName`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeDescription`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeSnapshot`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeType`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeMetadata`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeSchedulerHints`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithCreateVolumeFields`

- CreateVolume maps required Size and optional Image reference with default enabled fresh available waiting, unlimited SDK deadline, two-second interval and exact error failure policy. Cinder POST accepts only202; explicit Size, including0 or negative, always overrides any attribute size rather than being omitted. No initial volume, snapshot/source/backup lookup, Nova server creation or attachment operation is introduced.
  Tests: `blockstorage/create_volume_contracts_test.go:TestCreateVolumeContractsDefaultFreshWaitAndIndependentOwnedResults`, `blockstorage/create_volume_contracts_test.go:TestCreateVolumeContractsNoWaitSizeAndLiteralSourcesAreOwnedWithoutLookups`, `blockstorage/create_volume_options_test.go:TestCreateVolumeOptionsEffectiveDefaultsAndBootableForcesWaitAfterReplacement`.
- Optional image ID is captured and used without a Glance client or request; image Name requires Glance and resolves once through the existing exact Collection before Cinder creation. Missing/ambiguous/unsafe image identity aborts creation, the selected imageRef overrides normalized attributes and later caller reference mutation cannot change the selection.
  Tests: `blockstorage/create_volume_contracts_test.go:TestCreateVolumeContractsImageNameResolvesOnceAndReferenceWinsAllAttributes`, `blockstorage/create_volume_contracts_test.go:TestCreateVolumeContractsNoWaitSizeAndLiteralSourcesAreOwnedWithoutLookups`, `connection_create_volume_test.go:TestConnectionCreateVolumeCachedCinderLiveTokenAndOwnedImageSelection`, `resource/resolve_test.go:TestResolveIDUsesExactNamesAndNeverFetchesExplicitIDs`.
- The owned attribute schema covers all34 pinned direct Cinder v3 Volume Body declarations and inherited id/name/metadata by Python attribute or exact wire key, with source_replica an explicit Go-native extension. Unknown attributes and base_path/microversion/private route controls fail before lookup/POST. Caller request builders are unnecessary.
  Tests: `blockstorage/create_volume_contracts_test.go:TestCreateVolumeContractsAllPinnedAttributesAndWireAliasesAreSerialized`, `blockstorage/create_volume_contracts_test.go:TestCreateVolumeContractsPreflightValidatesEveryProvidedSourceBeforeCallbacks`, `blockstorage/create_volume_options_test.go:TestCreateVolumeOptionsValidateKnownFieldBoundaryInactiveWaitAndCancellation`.
- Alias mapping preserves source_volume_id/source_volid, image_id/imageRef, consistency_group_id/consistencygroup_id, bool names and host/project/migration/replication extension wires. When both attribute alias and wire key exist the wire key wins deterministically; nonnil concrete common attributes replace corresponding raw canonical values. Literal raw JSON values keep false/zero/null/empty and large numeric precision rather than applying Python descriptor coercion.

- Canonical name and description presence wins over display_name/display_description; selected null,false,numeric-zero,empty string/array/object is omitted and aliases never leak into the body. Required input Size and selected Image still override the resulting volume envelope independently of attribute order.

- String Metadata nil omits, nonnil empty emits{}, and raw Fields metadata:null is retained. Scheduler hints accept an object or null; null/empty object is omitted while a nonempty object moves to the top-level OS-SCH-HNT:scheduler_hints key outside volume without losing raw values, duplicates within arrays or unknown numeric precision.

- Whole/attribute/wait replacement factories and scalar/map helpers own pointers, maps, slices and raw JSON at factory capture and after each ordered callback. Retained callback configuration or reusable caller data cannot change the prepared execution. All inactive wait, JSON, attribute and selected source policies are validated before lookup or mutation, preserving nil versus empty behavior.

Connection validates context/receiver/image reference, prepares before cached Cinder/required Glance discovery, and delegates with that policy without rerunning originals or holding a mutex through callback phases. Explicit image ID or absent Image selects no Glance.

- Only the actual lower-case canonical creation id, validated as a nonempty safe segment, binds VolumeID and future GET/action paths. Canonical nonnull status is required; actual literal lowercase initial error fails immediately even with waiting disabled, while differently cased ERROR follows the separate no-wait or waiter semantics. Response aliases, request attribute id, foreign Location or later response id cannot retarget the workflow.

- Any nonnil Bootable forces effective waiting regardless of Wait option order. False waits but sends no false bootable action; true sends fixed POST200 os-set_bootable true only after a fresh successful available observation. Omitted Bootable with Wait(false) returns after valid202 creation without polling or an action.

- Waiting begins after creation response reading, Close, canonical identity binding and source validation. Nil/zero Timeout is unlimited and negative/inactive-invalid policy fails before mutation. Positive context deadline cancels Cinder poll HTTP and timers; parent context bounds every phase. Target available is tested before exact case-insensitive failures, no error-prefix inference occurs and nonnil empty failure states disable matching.

- Nonterminal ProgressCallback receives zero rather than arbitrary response progress extensions. Its error, source change or caller cancellation preserves inspectable callback/context/custom causes and prior Created plus LastAccepted proof. Target/failure observations suppress callback and source/context violations prevent another poll or action.

- Created and LastAccepted capture independent actual accepted202/200 raw bytes, cloned headers and status before read/Close/decode/post-source failures. Ready independently owns the successful available VolumeInfo. Later rejected HTTP/transport failures preserve native physical status/body/header/method/URL or original causes without overwriting prior LastAccepted or fabricating accepted evidence. Accepted processing failures never start a new logical creation or cleanup.

- VolumeInfo atomically decodes exact canonical nullable string/int/flag fields, literal timestamps, inherited links, raw MetadataFields and reused attachment records. Bootable/encrypted accept bool or exact case-insensitive true/false strings; other typed flags require JSON bool. - BootableSet retains actual opaque POST200 body/header/status on action acknowledgement or accepted handling errors without requiring JSON decoding, rewriting Ready.IsBootable, fetching again or certifying a later bootable observation. The action uses the original caller context after the wait-only budget has ended; action failure leaves Created/LastAccepted/Ready and its own native or accepted proof intact.

- Provided Cinder/Glance sources are captured before original package options, keep effective prefixed bases, canonical ordinary entry headers and consistent service role/microversion, and use original live providers for authentication. Provider/Endpoint/ResourceBase/Type/Microversion replacements are terminal after callbacks, lookup or phase handling. Configured native reauthentication/backoff/retry remain separate from SDK method/URL/serialized-body/accepted-policy ownership guards.

- Connection cache reuse, optional Glance selection, local preparation failures before discovery and service-initialization cancellation/custom causes are exposed through CreateVolume/volume operation errors. Existing native Cinder Create/SetBootable APIs, generated inventory, attachment workflows and Collection error-prefix waiter remain independent; no dependency declaration is promoted by this direct cloud-helper review.

Python/Go differences:

- Go uses an explicit optional resource.Ref Image instead of Python image dict/Resource/string identity polymorphism; ID requires no Glance request/client and Name resolves exactly once. Snapshot/source volume/backup attrs remain literal IDs with no extra lookup in either helper. SourceReplica is a declared native Go extension, not a missing pinned Python Body field.
- Go covers known Cinder attribute/wire names with concrete common controls and owned raw JSON. It deliberately preserves chosen JSON values without Python int/bool/BoolStr/dict/list descriptor coercion and rejects unknown/private/base_path/microversion controls instead of changing the selected route/session. Wire keys win duplicate alias representations deterministically because Go maps have no Python insertion-order contract; canonical name/description presence and falsey omission still match the cloud helper.
- Go fixes every follow-up route to a validated canonical creation id and requires usable canonical status, rejecting unsafe/missing/contradictory response identity without adopting request id, alternate identity or Location. Rich nullable models and raw precision are independent from mutable Python Resource hydration/descriptors, and fields without public typed projection remain in Metadata.Body.
- Go always fetches a fresh available observation after creation when effective waiting is enabled; Python may short-circuit a created Resource already in the target cached state. Both apply the literal initial lowercase error check even with wait disabled. Both nonnull bootable values force waiting, false sends no action and true sends a true action after readiness; Go preserves opaque action proof without synthesizing observed bootable state.
- Go intentionally uses nil/numeric-zero Timeout as unlimited waiting and rejects negative/inactive-invalid policies before mutation. Python forwards timeout and its iterate_timeout treats numeric zero/negative as immediate post-creation timeout unless an already-target Resource short-circuits. Positive Go wait context interrupts HTTP/timers while Python checks between fetches. The bootable action uses the original parent context after the wait-only phase, and caller context bounds the full Go workflow.
- Go returns Created/LastAccepted/Ready/BootableSet evidence and original errors after accepted side effects rather than Python returning one mutable Volume or raising without phase results. Error-returning progress callbacks, explicit failure controls and local preparation are Go extensions; no automatic delete or second logical create is added. Accepted phases are not a complete physical-attempt trace.
- Connection prepares original callbacks once before cached service selection and chooses Glance only for image Name; package calls capture every supplied source before originals. Configured current authentication and native retry/reauthentication remain in place while source and request ownership are guarded. This is not a packet-level exactly-once promise or arbitrary mutable provider configuration contract.
- This mapping closes only the directly declared cloud create_volume size/wait/timeout/image/bootable/known-kwargs workflow under documented Go policies. Separate Python Cinder create/wait/set_bootable, Glance find, inherited Resource/session/cache/API-version declarations retain independent reviews. Connection selects Cinder v3 and this row does not assert full Cinder v2 Resource object semantics or whole-SDK completion.

### `python:cloud/_block_storage/BlockStorageCloudMixin/delete_volume`

Status: `go_mapping`.

- Captured effective Cinder bases, selected type/microversion and ordinary entry headers remain fixed while original provider authentication is live. Configured native reauthentication/backoff/retry remain provider-owned while fixed-request guards reject changes to logical method/URL/query/serialized body or accepted response policy; SDK does not orchestrate replay or rollback.

  Tests: `connection_delete_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDeleteVolumeCachedCinderLiveTokenAndNoWaitStillLocates`, `connection_delete_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDeleteVolumeOptionsPrecedeDiscoveryAndReuseSelectedForceProtocol`, `connection_delete_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDeleteVolumePreflightSelectsNoService`, `connection_delete_volume_test.go:TestConnectionDeleteVolumeInitializationRetainsCancellationCause`, `blockstorage/create_volume_models_test.go:TestCreateVolumeModelsKnownFieldFailuresAreAtomicAndNullEmptyRemainDistinct`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go replaces ambiguous Python name_or_id strings with explicit resource.Ref. IDs use only an initial member GET; Names use exact Collection resolution and then a fresh fixed-ID confirmation GET. Python Resource.find first tries member GET and can fall back after400/403/404 to a detail list matching ID or name. The extra Go confirmation shifts initial absence after a name-list race: Go can returnfalse where Python would ignore a later mutation404 and returntrue.
- Go branches on the selected Cinder Microversion, with strict canonical3.<minor> validation and3.23 as the threshold, rather than Python utils.supports_microversion endpoint/default support discovery. Connection may discover a configured latest/range before helper capture; direct helper does no discovery or fallback. Empty Type is normalized on the copied client for consistent native version headers.
- Go normal DELETE always includes canonical lowercase cascade=false and selected modern force=true/false. Legacy Force sends pinned Python os-force_delete:null rather than native Gophercloud empty string. Go admits only the documented/native finite mutation status sets plus clean404, preserves opaque responses and rejects arbitrary route/query/body/session controls; existing native leaves retain independent behavior.
- Both helpers default to waiting forever after deletion rather than proxy standalone120s. Go nil/numeric-zero Timeout is unlimited and negative/inactive-invalid policy is rejected before HTTP. Python iterate_timeout treats numeric-zero/negative wait as immediate post-mutation timeout before the first deletion fetch. Positive Go context deadlines interrupt HTTP/timers while Python checks elapsed time between fetches; caller context additionally controls lookup and mutation.
- Go fresh200 observations require matching safe canonical IDs and strict known nullable types. Missing/null/empty status continues with no failure predicate; pinned Resource.wait_for_delete invokes resource.status.lower() when the Resource has status and may fail forNone. Python falsy fetch and mutable cached Resource behavior are represented by explicit Go404/deleted proof rather than arbitrary object truthiness or seeded hydration.
- Python returns one bool or raises. Go exposes Found/Deleted plus independently owned Located/Deletion/LastAccepted/Absent/Ready evidence even after accepted processing errors. Waitfalse Deletedtrue denotes completed request handling, not an observed absence. GET404 is independent absence evidence; mutation404 is retained only as Deletion proof. Error-returning callback0, raw nullable models and local option preparation are explicit Go extensions.
- Go only treats a clean direct logical Name miss or clean admitted physical404 as absence/race. Nested transport/native/list/read/Close/context/source failures remain errors with original causes. Captured client settings and fixed request ownership preserve current native authentication/retry without claiming packet-level exactly-once, arbitrary provider mutation, cleanup or rollback.

  Tests: `internal/cloudfilter/identifier_test.go:TestCloudFilterIdentifiersUsePythonJSONDomainStringAndContainerRepresentation`, `internal/cloudfilter/identifier_test.go:TestCloudFilterMissingAndNullIdentifiersDifferFromEmptyStringsAndRemainCaseSensitive`.
- Raw filters are consumed only after complete list and identifier phases. Omitted/null and ordinary falsey JSON filters return selected rows; truthy nonmapping filters remain lazy on empty selections. Invalid full syntax/UTF8 still errors after an empty identifier result, without an old physical ResponseError.
  Tests: `internal/cloudfilter/mapping_test.go:TestCloudFilterNonmappingFilterShapeIsLazyAfterIdentifierAndActualTruthiness`, `internal/cloudfilter/mapping_test.go:TestCloudFilterInvalidJSONIsLocalAtomicAndStillValidatedAfterEmptyIdentifierSelection`, `blockstorage/volume_search_test.go:TestSearchVolumesNormalizesEveryRowBeforeNextAndConsumesFiltersAfterFullList`.
- Filter keys retain raw insertion order and duplicate last-value/first-position semantics; earlier mismatches hide later missing keys. Nested objects recurse as subsets only when consumed, checking actual truthiness before nested shape/membership, so empty nested objects match truthy values but not falsey ones. Leaves keep complete array/object equality and exact Python bool-number compatibility.
  Tests: `internal/cloudfilter/mapping_test.go:TestCloudFilterOrderedMappingConsumesOnlyReachedKeysAndKeepsDuplicateFirstPosition`, `internal/cloudfilter/mapping_test.go:TestCloudFilterNestedEmptyObjectsUseActualTruthinessBeforeShapeOrMissingKeys`, `internal/cloudfilter/mapping_test.go:TestCloudFilterLeafEqualityKeepsExactArrayShapeAndPythonBooleanNumberPrecision`.
- A nonempty filter JSON string uses full JMESPath, even after no identifier matches; parser and evaluated runtime/function errors propagate atomically and lazy unvisited projection/boolean branches remain unvisited. Value can be arbitrary object/array/string/number/bool/null; expressions never fabricate physical source rows, IDs or HTTP metadata.
  Tests: `internal/cloudfilter/results_test.go:TestCloudFilterExpressionBridgeReturnsArbitraryOwnedJSONWithoutInventedRowIndices`, `internal/cloudfilter/results_test.go:TestCloudFilterExpressionsStillParseEmptySelectionAndKeepRuntimeFailuresAtomic`, `blockstorage/volume_search_test.go:TestSearchAndGetVolumeExpressionsKeepDynamicValuesAndSingleFalseyElement`, `blockstorage/volume_search_test.go:TestSearchVolumesExpressionErrorsEvenWithoutIdentifierMatchesKeepOnlyPageEvidence`.
- The service view exposes all 38 known normalized keys and excludes unknown wire-only fields, preserving raw originals separately. Aliases are consumed in textual order before eager descriptor conversion, including hidden invalid earlier alias; list/dict/BoolStr/bool/size conversions retain source presence/null/untyped semantics without borrowed strict workflow-model extraction.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_search_view_test.go:TestVolumeSearchViewKnownNullsAliasesAndIndependentLocation`, `blockstorage/volume_search_view_test.go:TestVolumeSearchViewDescriptorConversionsAndSourceSize`, `blockstorage/volume_search_view_test.go:TestVolumeSearchViewEagerErrorsAndDiscardedAlias`, `blockstorage/volume_search_mapping_boundaries_test.go:TestSearchVolumesMappingDuplicateWireKeysKeepTextualAliasOrderAndRawEvidence`.
- Computed independent row location uses current recorded scope plus known configuration, preserves raw zone and truthy foreign project ID, clears current project human names for foreign rows, and ignores wire location. Connection config, direct/adopted unknown facts and owned per-call complete overrides need no caller adapter or location lookup request.
  Tests: `connection_volume_search_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSearchCachedCinderOriginalCallbacksOnceAndOwnedLocation`, `connection_volume_search_test.go:TestConnectionCloudLocationOverrideAndOperationLocationFactoriesOwnInputs`, `connection_location_test.go:TestConnectVolumeSearchCapturesAutomaticCloudRegionConfiguredProjectAndForeignOwner`, `resource/cloud_location_test.go:TestCloudLocationForeignProjectsAndRawZoneSourceTruthiness`, `internal/cloudlocation/provider_test.go:TestRecordedCloudLocationNativeScopeShapesNeedNoExpiryOrHTTP`, `connection_location_mapping_boundaries_test.go:TestConnectCloudLocationMappingIDScopeLosesNativeDomainFactsAndOwnedOverrideRestores`.
- Original callback slice/inputs/retained config/result bytes, raw rows, page proof and headers are independently owned; original callbacks run once in order and cannot replace a later option slot. Direct source/header capture precedes original callbacks while native authentication remains live; reusable owned options preserve lazy malformed filters for operation phase.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_search_test.go:TestSearchVolumesOwnedOptionsOriginalCallbacksAndSliceSnapshot`, `internal/cloudfilter/results_test.go:TestCloudFilterSelectOwnsViewsFiltersIndicesAndResultsBeforeGuardCallbacks`, `connection_volume_search_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSearchCachedCinderOriginalCallbacksOnceAndOwnedLocation`.
- Preflight nil/canceled context, wrong/missing service role/source, nil options and terminal callback source mutation/cancel prevent HTTP and later callbacks. Connection applies originals before cached Cinder getter and propagates explicit cancellation cause without selecting a service on option failure.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_search_test.go:TestSearchVolumesPreflightContextOptionsAndFixedSourceRejectBeforeHTTP`, `connection_volume_search_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSearchOptionsBeforeGetterAndNilCancellationPreventSelection`, `internal/cloudfilter/results_test.go:TestCloudFilterGuardInterruptsTraversalAndRetainsCancellationCauseWithoutPartialSelection`.
- Native continuation is authoritative only for exact canonical volumes_links; same-origin escaped collection path is fixed, physical query spelling remains advertised and canonical query-cycle equivalence cannot restart traversal. Empty volumes[] ends before unused unsafe next; physical malformed/UTF8/null envelope or read/Close failures retain actual admitted page without Value/Volumes.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_search_transport_test.go:TestSearchVolumesContinuationAuthorityCyclesAndEmptySourceEOF`, `blockstorage/volume_search_transport_test.go:TestSearchVolumesAcceptedReadCloseEnvelopeAndUTF8ErrorsKeepPhysicalProof`.
- Later native HTTP/transport/cancellation errors never replace their actual cause with old accepted evidence; earlier admitted Pages remain independent. Cancellation while blocked continuation preserves cause and prior Pages, and all five source facts mutated after response are terminal with current admitted proof and no next exchange.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_search_transport_test.go:TestSearchVolumesLaterNativeAndTransportErrorsNeverBorrowAcceptedPage`, `blockstorage/volume_search_transport_test.go:TestSearchVolumesBlockedContinuationCancellationKeepsPriorPageAndCause`, `blockstorage/volume_search_transport_test.go:TestSearchVolumesPostResponseSourceMutationIsTerminalWithCurrentEvidence`.
- The shared physical reader retains native 401 reauthentication,429 backoff and503 retry behavior with live token, owned snapshot headers/microversion, fixed bodyless route and original200 policy; hook body/response ownership violations and expanded non200 acceptance cannot produce admitted helper success.
  Tests: `blockstorage/get_volumes_test.go:TestGetVolumesNativeAuthBackoffRetryKeepsBodylessExactListAndLiveHeaders`.
- Repaired unchanged pinned Python AST reference cases retain values and meaningful lazy error phases; exact-decimal truthiness and local guard interruptions are deliberate Go boundaries rather than source error swallowing.
  Tests: `internal/cloudfilter/reference_test.go:TestCloudFilterRepairedPinnedReferenceSnapshotsRetainValuesAndErrorPhases`, `internal/cloudfilter/results_test.go:TestCloudFilterPythonTruthyPreservesExactDecimalZeroPolicyAndNullableResults`.
- The full JMESPath fork retains all26 standard functions and grammar composition, arbitrary values, lazy unvisited branches and propagated evaluated child errors; an independently generated146-case fixture separates110 selected Python runtime goldens from36 explicit Go ordering/numeric/grammar boundaries, with every case checking input immutability. Literal numbers stay json.Number; compiled literal results are deeply independent and concurrent compiled evaluation cannot inherit result mutation. Corrected syntax rejects third-colon slices and declared malformed repeated bounds/function comma spellings.
  Tests: `internal/jmespath/engine_test.go:TestSearchReferenceAndDeclaredBoundaries`, `internal/jmespath/engine_test.go:TestSearchLiteralNumberRepresentation`, `internal/jmespath/engine_test.go:TestCompiledLiteralResultOwnership`, `internal/jmespath/engine_test.go:TestCompiledSearchConcurrent`, `internal/jmespath/engine_test.go:TestCompiledLiteralConcurrentOwnership`, `internal/jmespath/engine_test.go:TestLexerSyntaxBoundaries`.

Python/Go differences:

- Only the two direct BlockStorageCloudMixin declarations search_volumes and get_volume are proposed here, pinned to openstacksdk ef55d7d1666099f50bf1e1c40b59d7e7b72a51fe and native Gophercloud v2.15.0. Related list_volumes/get_volumes/get_volume_id/volume_exists, proxy/resource/native APIs and full SDK completeness retain independent review status.
- The public typed NameOrID string replaces Python duck-typed call values and None search input; empty search string means no local identifier restriction. JSON response id/name preserve untyped values and are stringified with Python-style null/bool/ordered-container representation. Identifier floating spellings use Go shortest float64 rendering at Python cutoffs, including overflow inf and underflow signed zero; exact CPython float repr and Unicode printability database behavior are explicit representation boundaries.
- The source mutable Volume Resource/Munch and class descriptors are represented by an owned normalized 38-key JSON view plus independent original RawResource wire evidence. The view covers the 34 direct v3 Body members, inherited id/name/metadata and computed location used by these helpers, without implementing mutable Resource identity, custom mappings/equality, arbitrary Python objects, plugin/session cache, resource mutation or arbitrary inherited APIs. Python typing.cast does not constrain expression results; Go Value likewise permits any ordinary JSON shape.
- Ordinary mapping equality and location project equality keep Python bool-number recursive compatibility but compare mathematical JSON decimals exactly rather than binary-float JSON rounding. Ordinary truthiness also uses exact mantissa zero; for example 1e-9999 stays truthy rather than underflowing to Python float zero. JMESPath scalar boolean-number equality is distinct, container/contains equality follows the selected Python reference, and JMESPath numeric zero stays truthy.
- The engine is a licensed adapted source fork of official go-jmespath v0.4.0 with full grammar and all 26 standard functions. Independently selected jmespath.py 1.0.1 is a comparison reference satisfying SDK jmespath>=0.9.0, not an installed-version SDK pin or a claim that every version in that range has identical runtime behavior. Custom Python functions/classes and nonordinary JSON values are outside this API representation.
- JMESPath equality/order/min/max/sort/by-key preserve exact decimals, including large integers and arbitrary exponent text. Exact abs/ceil/floor and integral sum differ from Python float spelling/rounding; nonintegral sum/avg/floating to_number use finite IEEE-754 conversion and return errors on nonfinite results. Integer arithmetic needing more than 1048576 coefficient digits errors instead of allocating unbounded memory. Numeric ordering never rounds or expands exponent-sized coefficients.
- JMESPath object keys/values/object projections/JSON serialization use deterministic lexical key order, replacing Python dictionary insertion order for expression evaluation. Raw cloud filter objects retain original key order and duplicate last-value at first insertion position. Original nested wire JSON order is retained for identifier container representation; normalized top-level Volume descriptor order follows source MRO.
- Response aliases and duplicate exact response keys use textual last-consumed-wins. Python JSON dictionary duplicate collapse preserves first insertion position before Resource alias consumption, so duplicates interleaved with distinct aliases can produce a different value. This is an explicit ambiguous-wire Go policy, not an assertion of every possible duplicate-key Python response equivalence.
- Volume size uses source int descriptor behavior: bool remains bool, JSON integers are arbitrary precision, finite float JSON truncates its actual float64 value, decimal digit strings convert, signed/padded/nondigit strings and nonnumeric containers become zero, and digit-only nondecimal strings fail. The volume descriptor uses audited Unicode16 tables and does not emulate CPython configurable decimal string digit limits. JMESPath numeric-string digits and identifier printability follow Go Unicode tables; ordinary UTF-8/JSON parser and byte syntax offsets differ from arbitrary Python Unicode objects.
- Computed location never trusts HTTP location: Connection retains known selected cloud/region/auth name/domain facts and the provider recorded native token project scope; row project/zone are raw JSON values with foreign-project names cleared. Direct ServiceClient calls and FromProvider manual/custom auth results cannot reconstruct absent cloud/configuration facts and leave them null unless explicit owned options supply them. Recorded scope does not force authentication refresh; native HTTP reauthentication remains live.
- Native clouds.Parse discards configured project-domain metadata when selecting project ID scope and collapses absent versus empty configuration strings. Go cannot reconstruct those facts from returned AuthOptions or token names; WithCloudLocation/WithVolumeSearchLocation are explicit complete owned overrides. This configuration parser representation boundary is independent from the direct helper body control flow.
- The library owns concrete option callbacks/defaults rather than requiring caller builders. Direct calls capture source facts/header snapshot before original callbacks; Connection applies originals once before cached Cinder selection, then direct delegation snapshots the resulting source. Retained config pointers, callback-slice slot edits and ordinary MoreHeaders edits cannot change prepared owned options. Provider authentication stays live; source fact mutation/cancellation is terminal at checked boundaries.
- Go retains only actually admitted response Body/Header/StatusCode in independent Pages/Observed. Accepted read/Close/schema/source/context errors keep that physical proof without SDK replay; rejected native HTTP/transport failures retain their own native error and never borrow an older accepted page. Safe same-origin/same escaped collection continuation and cycle checks intentionally reject unsafe routes instead of following Python session pagination beyond those guards. Empty arrays terminate before unused continuation links are inspected.
- Go errors are OperationError with actual operation/resource and original causes; local consumed filter/selection errors wrap ErrInvalidOption, ambiguity wraps ErrAmbiguous, and no fabricated HTTP/NotFound evidence is attached. Python deprecation warnings and diagnostic log messages are not reproduced. Concurrent mutation of caller mutable source/config/model data is outside the race-safe contract; independent-result ownership does not claim such mutations are safe.
- Standard function arity and types are validated for every variadic argument, so merge requires all arguments to be objects; selected Python1.0.1 can accept some iterable key/value pairs through dict.update. Standard grammar rejects repeated slice bounds without intervening colons, omitted function commas and trailing commas that the selected Python parser permissively accepts. These stricter malformed-input boundaries are declared rather than falsely reported as matching every Python parser spelling. All valid grammar branches and26 standard function names remain provided.
- Location timing is explicit: Connection defaults snapshot recorded current scope after original option callbacks and before service selection; the owned operation Location is not rewritten by a later native reauthentication scope change. A direct ServiceClient with no Location override reads recorded scope when each row is normalized. Python current_project_id may refresh session authentication during Resource location construction; Go does not add such location-triggered I/O.

- Native memberGET supports original401 reauthentication,429 backoff and503 general retry while keeping fixed bodyless route, live provider token, pre-option headers and microversion, callbacks once and a single final admitted proof. - Returned normalized JSON, original wire resource metadata and accepted member/list proof are independent snapshots; original options/location ownership and cached Cinder selection work through Connection with originals exactly once and no hidden name/location/auth lookup.

### `python:cloud/_block_storage/BlockStorageCloudMixin/list_volumes`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk.Connection.ListVolumes`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.ListVolumes`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.PrepareVolumeReadOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithVolumeReadOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.WithVolumeReadLocation`

- ListVolumes is the direct cloud complete detailed list. Its initial bodyless GET200 is /volumes/detail without identity/status/all-project/page/filter controls; complete materialization returns all source rows in order and never performs member GETs or name resolution. The deprecated Python cache argument is warning-only/no-op and is omitted in Go.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_reads_contract_test.go:TestListVolumesMaterializesAllPagesAndOwnsNormalizedAndWireRows`, `connection_volume_reads_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeReadsCachedCinderOriginalOptionsPrecedeSelection`.
- Every raw row is converted through the complete eager normalized v3 descriptor/location view while consuming that row, before any continuation is inspected or requested. Malformed visited descriptor/location errors stop the collector; a later page error never exposes earlier partial Value/Volumes.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_reads_contract_test.go:TestListVolumesEagerDescriptorFailureStopsBeforeNextAndDiscardsPartialValues`, `blockstorage/volume_reads_contract_test.go:TestVolumeExistsAmbiguityStopsBeforeUnusedTailThatFullListMustConsume`, `blockstorage/volume_reads_transport_test.go:TestListVolumesLaterNativeOrTransportErrorKeepsOnlyActualPriorPages`, `blockstorage/volume_search_test.go:TestSearchVolumesNormalizesEveryRowBeforeNextAndConsumesFiltersAfterFullList`.
- Value is an owned JSON array of normalized resources and Volumes is the corresponding owned raw source-row slice. Empty success is nonnull [] plus a nonnil empty slice. Duplicate IDs remain separate original rows/pointers in wire order; unrelated unknown fields and exact numeric text remain in RawResource.Body rather than being fabricated into the normalized view.

Nonempty pages use exact native volumes_links continuation extraction with same collection escaped path/origin and cycle guards. Source-advertised queries are retained; no HTTP Link/data.next/marker fallback, local pagination restart or page cap is invented.

- Go selects Cinder v3 under Gophercloud v2.15.0 and uses the audited 38-field normalized Volume view plus an independent RawResource physical row and response proof. Pinned cloud can select v2; this row does not implement a v2 projection or promote native/Proxy/Resource/session/cache declarations.
- Complete canonical UTF-8 volume(s) envelopes and accepted200 responses are the deliberate Go decoder boundary. Python generic Resource translation can tolerate invalid JSON on member fetch, unwrap absent envelopes more permissively, wrap nonarray resource lists, strip self, and preserve seeded omitted IDs. Go raw evidence preserves unknown/self fields while normalized known fields exclude them.

Exact decimal raw precision, Unicode/version and configurable Python numeric conversion boundaries are inherited from the already audited normalized view; no Python object identity, mutable Resource lifecycle or warning subsystem is recreated.
- Go returns richer owned result/evidence structures with an error; rejected native status/body/header remain in their original error. Admitted phase proof survives later failures without establishing successful resource/boolean output, and no SDK orchestration replay or swallowed cancellation is introduced.
- Pinned list_volumes(cache=None) warns for every non-None cache value, including False, without changing list behavior. The Go API deliberately has no obsolete cache/warning parameter and exposes none of the lower Proxy query/details/all_projects controls.
- Go continuation policy maps native canonical volumes_links only. Pinned generic Resource.list can consult links/data.next/HTTP Link and limited marker fallback; this direct workflow does not promote those generic capabilities.
- Local caller Location errors are separate from physical response/descriptor errors: actual admitted proof remains inspectable, but a local error is not reclassified as a200 response failure. This preserves the existing shared GetVolume/SearchVolumes normalizer behavior.

- Deprecated get_extra is a warning-only no-op in pinned list_volume_types and is omitted from Go; no extra-spec enrichment is lost. Go does not reproduce warning delivery.
- Go selects Cinder v3 under Gophercloud v2.15.0. Only this actual cloud helper is mapped; independent v2 Type, native/Proxy declarations, generic Resource/session/cache, access and mutation profiles are not promoted by this row.

- Go public aliases and repeated wire keys consume textual order and the last value wins. Python json.loads collapses repeated keys at their first insertion position before alias descriptor consumption; interleaved duplicate/alias inputs can differ. Existing exact mathematical JSON truthiness also keeps 1e-9999 nonzero whereas ordinary Python float decoding can underflow to false.
- List rows with no recognized Body key preserve canonical wire location, including false/null/arbitrary JSON; known-key presence discards it and member translation always ignores wire computed location. Go supplies owned current scope or a complete override, retaining override Zone and performing no unknown owner/zone inference. Local caller Location failures remain separate from physical response failures.
- Usable HTTP Link fallback is a declared Go repair: pinned Resource._get_next_link reads response.links[next][uri] but ordinary Requests returns url. Existing quoted Link parsing handles multiple angle targets, quoted delimiters, case-insensitive/multi-relation next and last-next replacement across headers. Native canonical singular volume_type_links is an explicit lower-priority compatibility extension; Python derives plural volume_types_links.
- Next query uses source-style nonblank parse_qs merge, with old marker/limit removed and omitted nonpagination keys retained; explicit advertised keys replace current keys. Go encodes one merged query and preserves effective advertised paths instead of copying source relative /v prefix stripping or query-only URL-plus-params duplication. No initial caller limit or invented numeric marker fallback is exposed.
- Strict canonical UTF-8 envelopes and accepted200, fragment/userinfo/opaque/query/origin/escaped-path checks and complete-URL cycle detection are Go safety boundaries. Source Resource.list permits other shapes and has separate same-marker bookkeeping, so these rules are intentional mappings rather than generic pagination equivalence.
- Go exposes independently owned normalized values, raw resources and actual physical response proof with errors and joined cancellation causes. Native protected request retry is retained; SDK workflow replay, mutable session negotiation and cached object lifecycle are separate scope.

Go uses safe single-segment exact-routing policy for slash/backslash/query/fragment/percent/Unicode whitespace names and retains only fresh canonical response ID/public fields, producing null when absent rather than fabricating the seed.
- Go returns nullable Value plus optional actual Type and Observed/Pages proof. Python returns Resource/None or arbitrary expression first values; Go distinguishes absence from a selected false/zero/empty string and exposes a library-owned VolumeTypeSelectionError wrapping ErrAmbiguous instead of an SDKException class.
- Go selects Cinder v3 under Gophercloud v2.15.0. Only this actual cloud helper is mapped; independent v2 Type, native/Proxy declarations, generic Resource/session/cache, access and mutation profiles are not promoted by this row.
- The six-field view follows v3 Type descriptors, with unknown original wire fields retained only in RawResource. Go owns raw JSON and exact numeric text; this is not a claim that a plain Go map reproduces the complete mutable Python Resource runtime.
- Go public aliases and repeated wire keys consume textual order and the last value wins. Python json.loads collapses repeated keys at their first insertion position before alias descriptor consumption; interleaved duplicate/alias inputs can differ. Existing exact mathematical JSON truthiness also keeps 1e-9999 nonzero whereas ordinary Python float decoding can underflow to false.
- List rows with no recognized Body key preserve canonical wire location, including false/null/arbitrary JSON; known-key presence discards it and member translation always ignores wire computed location. Go supplies owned current scope or a complete override, retaining override Zone and performing no unknown owner/zone inference. Local caller Location failures remain separate from physical response failures.
- Usable HTTP Link fallback is a declared Go repair: pinned Resource._get_next_link reads response.links[next][uri] but ordinary Requests returns url. Existing quoted Link parsing handles multiple angle targets, quoted delimiters, case-insensitive/multi-relation next and last-next replacement across headers. Native canonical singular volume_type_links is an explicit lower-priority compatibility extension; Python derives plural volume_types_links.
- Next query uses source-style nonblank parse_qs merge, with old marker/limit removed and omitted nonpagination keys retained; explicit advertised keys replace current keys. Go encodes one merged query and preserves effective advertised paths instead of copying source relative /v prefix stripping or query-only URL-plus-params duplication. No initial caller limit or invented numeric marker fallback is exposed.
- Strict canonical UTF-8 envelopes and accepted200, fragment/userinfo/opaque/query/origin/escaped-path checks and complete-URL cycle detection are Go safety boundaries. Source Resource.list permits other shapes and has separate same-marker bookkeeping, so these rules are intentional mappings rather than generic pagination equivalence.
- Go exposes independently owned normalized values, raw resources and actual physical response proof with errors and joined cancellation causes. Native protected request retry is retained; SDK workflow replay, mutable session negotiation and cached object lifecycle are separate scope.

- Resolve one ordinary unfiltered exact volume through the captured reader, retaining the selected original raw JSON row. Bind only the actual safe canonical returned string ID and retain Resolved on local target errors.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestVolumeMutationsOrdinaryFindFallbackUsesNameQueryAndActualReturnedID`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestVolumeMutationsCompletedAbsenceAndSecondMatchAmbiguityStopBeforeMutation`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_transport_test.go:TestVolumeMutationLookupSchemaAndUnexpectedNativeStatusStopBeforeMutation`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestVolumeMutationsRejectInvalidActualCanonicalIDLocallyWithResolvedProof`, `connection_volume_mutations_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeMutationsLocalTargetAndLocationFailuresKeepLookupWithoutMutation`, `connection_volume_mutations_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeMutationsOriginalsPrecedeCachedCinderAndBindActualResponseID`.
- Supply every writable field from the actual37 Volume Body descriptors through SDK-owned typed/raw options. Concrete fields override raw Fields; Go map wire keys win normalized aliases. Canonical name/description presence wins display aliases and selected falsey values are dropped. Preserve nullable/empty raw fields, ignore unknown inputs and source_replica, reject id/runtime route controls, and inject no creation defaults.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeForwardsAllKnownBodyAttributesWithoutCreationDefaults`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeWireAliasesAndTypedAttributesHaveDeterministicPrecedence`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeDropsFalseyCloudNamesAndIgnoresUnknownAttributes`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_options_test.go:TestVolumeMutationPreflightContextRoleCallbacksAndProtectedFieldsStopBeforeHTTP`.
- Compare requested canonical raw values with selected wire-order prior state, using recursive Python boolean/number equality, object order independence, ordered lists and the declared exact-decimal boundary. Equal assignments retain original raw values; absent differs explicit null. Empty/equal/unknown-only updates skip PUT and leave Applied nil with independently owned logical lookup-derived results.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeNoOpPreservesOriginalRawRecursivePythonEquality`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeSendsOnlyDirtyFieldsForStringNumberListOrderAndExactDecimals`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeAbsentKnownFieldVersusExplicitNullIsDirty`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeDirtyComparisonConsumesLookupAliasesInActualWireOrder`, `connection_volume_mutations_test.go:TestConnectionUpdateVolumeNoOpKeepsLookupAndSkipsMutation`.
- Build the complete proposed descriptor/location view before PUT so invalid known conversions fail locally with Resolved and no Applied. Serialize dirty raw values without descriptor coercion. Move dirty scheduler hints outside volume only when truthy; dirty falsey hints still send volume{} and a nonobject raw hint may have an empty dictionary descriptor view.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeProposedDescriptorFailureIsLocalBeforePUT`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeSendsOnlyDirtyFieldsForStringNumberListOrderAndExactDecimals`, `blockstorage/volume_mutations_contract_test.go:TestUpdateVolumeSchedulerHintsDirtyFalseyValuesStillCommitEmptyVolume`, `connection_volume_mutations_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeMutationsLocalTargetAndLocationFailuresKeepLookupWithoutMutation`.
- Merge recognized fields from canonical volume or flat object responses into owned prior/request state; omitted fields remain and returned ID is only a result attribute.
