Python/Go differences:

- Only this actual cloud declaration is mapped to selected Cinder v3 under pinned Gophercloud v2.15.0. Independent native/Proxy/v2 operations, inherited Resource state/session/cache/microversion negotiation and other workflows retain separate support status; this row is not a full SDK completion claim.
- Go uses one fresh canonical lookup result and validates an actual safe nonempty string ID. Python can retain a seeded mutable Resource ID and utils.urljoin coercion, including missing/falsey or route-changing values; local Go target validation retains Resolved without borrowing HTTP200.
- Ordinary lookup reuses existing Go exact identity/name, safe list-only input routing, nullable descriptor/location view, fixed canonical GET200 and continuation policies. These declared mappings do not establish inherited generic Resource equivalence.
- Source v3 mutation raise_from_response status<400 maps to valid HTTP100..399, independently of lookup200 and native mutation200. Informational exposure follows net/http. Fixed origin/escaped-path/query/method guards intentionally block redirects that alter the bound request.
- Go adds independently owned lookup and mutation body/header/status evidence with joined native/read/Close/context causes and OperationError identity. Applied is acknowledgement evidence, not proof of changed/completed cloud state; rejected/local failures do not borrow prior success proof.
- Original SDK options are owned and applied once. Direct calls capture source before originals; Connection applies originals before CurrentLocation/getter and uses cached Cinder v3. Scope is frozen for the call while authentication/native retry stays live; no SDK workflow replay occurs.
- Python update checks configured proxy v3 after lookup and bootable can follow configured v2/v3. Go exposes the selected v3 contract, captures the chosen microversion, and does not perform generic session negotiation; a direct ServiceClient alone cannot prove configured API version.
- The actual cloud update has no valid_kwargs decorator and recognizes all37 Volume Body descriptors. Go accepts the36 nonidentity writable descriptors/aliases with raw nullable JSON; id/runtime route controls are rejected rather than mutating Resource identity/request policy. Unknown inputs are ignored, including creation-only source_replica.
- Input Go maps use deterministic wire-name precedence and concrete-field precedence; Python kwargs use insertion order. Selected lookup/response JSON uses last textual alias order, which differs from interleaved duplicate keys collapsed by Python JSON loading. Known fields compare and serialize raw, rather than normalized descriptor values.
- Go exact-decimal recursive boolean/number equality and truthiness differ from Python JSON float rounding/underflow and arbitrary Python objects. No-op equal assignment retains original raw fields. Ordinary JSON descriptor conversion reuses the declared search view rather than native typed Volume extraction.
- Source partial/flat response merge and empty/malformed JSON ValueError tolerance are preserved with independently owned prior/request/server state. Go rejects invalidUTF8 explicitly, retains prior unknown raw fields as proof extension, ignores unknown response fields, and commits logical Volume/Value atomically rather than exposing a partially mutated shared Resource.

  Tests: `internal/cloudsnapshot/model_test.go:TestSnapshotModelHasSixteenNullableFieldsAndPreservesUntypedValues`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/model_test.go:TestSnapshotModelBoolStrIsNullableWithoutAnImplicitFalseDefault`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_contract_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadListMaterializesAllPagesAndOwnsRawAndNormalizedRows`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotOriginalOptionsPrecedeCachedCinderSelectionForAllEntries`.
- Ordinary descriptor conversion is eager at Resource constructor/cache refresh: strict nullable BoolStr, Python bool/arbitrary integer preservation, parsed finite IEEE float truncation and Unicode decimal-string integer conversion, nullable ordinary metadata dictionary. Parsed duplicate keys keep first insertion position with final value; selected alias wins in dictionary traversal order and discarded alias is not converted.
  Tests: `internal/cloudsnapshot/model_test.go:TestSnapshotModelIntegerAndMetadataDescriptorsMatchOrdinaryResourceConversion`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/model_test.go:TestSnapshotModelAliasesUseParsedDuplicateCollapseAndFirstInsertionOrder`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_contract_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadListSelectedAliasConvertsOnlyFinalParsedDictionaryValue`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_contract_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadListEagerDescriptorsFailBeforeLocalExclusionAndContinuation`.
- Snapshot location is recomputed from the owned configured/authenticated scope and actual project descriptor, including empty/location-only rows. Falsey/equal project retains configured names; truthy foreign project keeps raw ID and clears names/domains. Zone is always null because Snapshot has no zone descriptor. Raw location/availability_zone cannot replace it; caller location errors remain local without fabricated response errors. Owned Location is validated before first HTTP, and Connection validates it before its Cinder getter.
  Tests: `internal/cloudsnapshot/model_test.go:TestSnapshotModelRecomputesLocationFromProjectAndAlwaysUsesNullZone`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/model_test.go:TestSnapshotModelRejectsPhysicalShapeAndLocalLocationFailuresAtomicallyAndOwnsResult`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotRecordedScopeIsCapturedAfterOptionsBeforeGetterAndRetainedAcrossPages`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotExplicitLocationIsOwnedAndZoneIgnoredWithoutChangingDefaults`.
- Prepare/factories own full raw Location, pointer and collection policy; complete options replace earlier policy. Original callbacks run exactly once in captured slice order with independent snapshots after each callback. Nil context/options, canceled custom cause and callback failures stop without partial prepared policy or HTTP.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_snapshot_options_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotLocationFactoriesOwnEveryNullableComponentAndAreReusable`, `blockstorage/volume_snapshot_options_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotWholeOptionsReplaceEarlierPolicyRatherThanMerging`, `blockstorage/volume_snapshot_options_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotOriginalsRunOnceInCapturedOrderAndRetainedConfigsCannotAlterLaterPolicy`, `blockstorage/volume_snapshot_options_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotPrepareRejectsNilAndCanceledContextBeforeOriginals`, `blockstorage/volume_snapshot_options_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotPrepareNilOptionAndCallbackFailuresStopWithoutPartialPolicy`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotOriginalSliceAndRetainedOptionPointersCannotChangePreparedPolicy`.
- Direct workflows capture provider/endpoint/ResourceBase/type/microversion before originals and retain sticky source violations; ordinary headers remain snapshots while auth tokens stay live. Connection executes originals once before only cached Cinder v3 selection and captures current/explicit location before getter. Local callback/getter/preflight errors retain outer operation and custom causes without inventing physical proof.
  Tests: `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadOriginalOptionsOwnEachSnapshotAndSourceOrdinaryHeaders`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadOriginalCallbackFailureJoinsSourceAndContextBeforeHTTP`, `internal/cloudread/policy_test.go:TestCloudReadPolicySourceChangeStaysStickyBeforeAnyPolicyCommit`, `internal/cloudread/policy_test.go:TestCloudReadPolicyCancellationRetainsCauseAndIsAtomic`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotOriginalOptionsPrecedeCachedCinderSelectionForAllEntries`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotPreflightOriginalAndGetterErrorsPreserveOuterOperationAndCause`.
- Each admitted physical page/member owns body/header/status; normalized Value, raw row and error proof own independent state. Read/Close/source drift/custom cancellation keep the actual current phase and all causes while logical outputs remain unpublished. Rejected later/blocked requests retain only earlier completed pages and never borrow an earlier successful or suppressed member response.
  Tests: `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadAcceptedReadCloseCancellationRetainsCurrentPhysicalStage`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadAcceptedSourceFactsAreFrozenThroughBodyClose`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadBlockedContinuationCancellationKeepsOnlyCompletedPage`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_selection_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadLookupFailureRetainsPagesButNeverSuppressedMemberProof`, `connection_volume_snapshots_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeSnapshotFailuresKeepOnlyActualPhysicalProofAndCustomCancellation`.
- Native reauth/retry remains live and bounded in fixtures while method/fixed route/query/bodyless decoder/body retention/original100..399 policy and source remain guarded. Expanded native400/403/404 is terminal with actual native status/body/header causes, not compatible fallback or admitted ResponseError. Redirect cannot forward credentials to an altered target. No SDK orchestration replay/wait/action/rollback is added.
  Tests: `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadNativeReauthRetryKeepLiveTokenAndOwnedSourceOnce`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadNativeExpandedFallbackStatusIsTerminalOriginalPolicyRejection`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadNativeRetryCannotReplaceBodyDecoderRetentionOrSource`, `blockstorage/snapshot_read_transport_test.go:TestVolumeSnapshotReadRedirectCannotSendTokenToChangedFixedTarget`, `internal/cloudread/policy_test.go:TestCloudReadPolicyOwnedWirePolicyKeepsLiveTokenAcrossNativeRetry`, `internal/cloudread/policy_test.go:TestCloudReadPolicySourceMutationInNativeRetryStopsBeforeResend`.

Python/Go differences:

- Only this actual direct BlockStorageCloudMixin helper is mapped using selected Cinder v3 and Gophercloud v2.15.0. Independent Proxy/native/v2/Resource/session/adapter/cache declarations retain separate reviews; full SDK is not complete.
- Python default/overloaded/runtime arguments map to explicit Go request types and owned concrete With/Prepare options, not caller-implemented interface builders. Go accepts finite ordinary JSON and typed controls rather than arbitrary mutable Python Resource/custom runtime objects.
- Logical normalized16-field nullable values, physical raw Resource/Observed/Pages and error proof are independently owned Go representations. Unknown raw fields and number spelling are evidence extensions; mutable Resource identity, stale dict cache and session memoization are not reproduced.
- Ordinary source descriptor behavior and parsed dictionary duplicate/alias order are preserved. Go Unicode16 decimal tables and arbitrary integer policy do not reproduce configurable CPython integer digit limits; exact decimal truthiness/equality differs from Python float rounding/underflow. Untyped timestamps/IDs/names stay JSON rather than native typed leaf extraction.
- Connected Snapshot location is recomputed including empty/location-only rows, using explicit/current recorded scope and raw project descriptor; zone remains null. Direct clients recover recorded auth without fabricating configured names. Go owns snapshots and rejects invalid policy locally rather than emulating dynamic Connection/session objects.
- Go captures original source facts and ordinary headers before originals on direct calls; Connection prepares originals before cached Cinder getter. Native auth remains live, sticky source/fixed request/cause guards and atomic logical publication are explicit Go safety/proof extensions; no SDK replay/wait/rollback is introduced.
- Source validHTTP100..399 and UTF8-valid empty/malformed member JSON tolerance are preserved instead of narrowing to native200. Go additionally requires complete valid UTF8 and JSON/object/envelope/descriptor shape on consumed data, preserves native rejected evidence and prevents expanded native>=400 from becoming success or fallback.
- Selected native microversion is preserved, including symbolic/empty settings when supplied. Source Snapshot3.65 limit only caps absent-default server-discovery negotiation; Go does not automatically negotiate/cap/upgrade a supplied client. Explicit Connection version/range negotiation remains independent.
- Search preserves complete detailed list-before-selection and the distinct full _filter_list path. NameOrID is a Go string (empty means omitted); nonstring raw row identifiers use ordinary JSON Python str/repr compatibility, full case-sensitive fnmatch translation and source order rather than filesystem matching.
- The full in-repo JMESPath engine accepts UseNumber JSON and returns arbitrary finite JSON, not a promised Snapshot array. Selected Python jmespath1.0.1 reference pin is a validation dependency satisfying SDK>=0.9.0, not a new SDK source pin. Exact decimal, lexical object order, stricter malformed grammar and arbitrary nonJSON/runtime-object boundaries are declared in engine documentation.
- Lazy ordered mapping/shape errors and nested Python bool-number equality are retained without narrowing expression outputs to rows. Go explicit Value/RawResource association distinguishes ordinary selected row indices from arbitrary expression values; local errors do not borrow completed HTTP status.

Accepted discovery HTTP200/300 proof is independent of import Applied. Malformed accepted data, joined rejected IO/callback faults, expanded OkCodes, five-fact source drift and custom cancellation are terminal and cannot authorize POST or fabricate admitted proof.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_discovery_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupDiscoveryIsFiniteAndCleanUnavailableFallsBack`, `blockstorage/backup_import_discovery_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupInvalidAcceptedDiscoveryKeepsOnlyCurrentDiscoveryProof`, `blockstorage/backup_import_discovery_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupDiscoveryFaultsSourceDriftAndCancellationAreTerminal`.
- A fresh disconnected nullable24 Backup has literal null location and never consumes CurrentLocation, configured/token project lookup, response zone/location or request-ID seed. Response ID remains untyped, including missing/null/false/zero/empty/container values. Known-only overlay shares ordinary Boolean/list/dict/integer descriptors; actual raw unknown/member fields remain independent evidence. Connection reuses cached Cinder and live token with captured ordinary headers, while each discovered microversion belongs to that call rather than the cached client.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_contract_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupBuildsDisconnectedNullableBackupFromActualKnownFields`, `blockstorage/backup_import_contract_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupKeepsArbitraryResponseIDWithoutInventingSeed`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/backup_disconnected_test.go:TestBackupDisconnectedModelKeepsNullLocationAndNeverSeedsIdentity`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/backup_disconnected_test.go:TestBackupDisconnectedModelSharesNullableDescriptorConversion`, `connection_volume_backup_import_test.go:TestConnectionImportVolumeBackupUsesCachedCinderAndIgnoresInvalidLocation`, `connection_volume_backup_import_test.go:TestConnectionImportVolumeBackupDiscoveryPolicyIsPerCallAndDoesNotMutateCache`.
- Present backup envelope wins; absent envelope selects a flat object. Ordinary empty/malformed UTF-8 JSON retains the all-null view without an invented raw object; genuine empty object remains actual evidence. Valid wrong shapes, invalid UTF-8 and descriptor conversion errors keep current accepted proof and no Value or decoder retry. A consumed physical response ID survives later descriptor failure; failures before overlay retain null.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_contract_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupUsesEnvelopePresenceAndOnlyMalformedJSONLeavesDefaults`.
- Publish logical Value only after translation, descriptor conversion and final captured-source guard. BackupID, Value, selected raw Backup, Applied bytes/headers and separate calls own independent storage. Native original-policy >=400 rejection has no Applied or logical success. Accepted Read/Close/source/context errors keep actual stage proof and independent ResponseError with all causes, close once, and do not replay; earlier admitted discovery is never borrowed as import Applied.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_contract_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupLogicalPhysicalAndSeparateCallsOwnIndependentStorage`, `blockstorage/backup_import_contract_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupHTTPRejectionNeverBecomesAppliedOrLogicalSuccess`, `blockstorage/backup_import_transport_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupAcceptedFaultsKeepAppliedAndEveryCauseWithoutValue`, `connection_volume_backup_import_test.go:TestConnectionImportVolumeBackupExecutionErrorsKeepOnlyActualStageProof`.
- Live native reauthentication and bounded caller retry preserve captured ordinary headers, source and owned serialized literal body. Mutable body/decoder/retention options, source changes or context interruption stop before resend with original and callback causes. Nonempty operation microversion owns consistent modern/legacy headers; explicit empty policy rejects injected versions. Retry map aliases may agree, while physical headers require one value per name.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_transport_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupLiveReauthAndBoundedRetryKeepLiteralOwnedBody`, `blockstorage/backup_import_transport_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupRetryMutationCannotSendChangedTargetBodyOrVersion`.
- Every physical POST, including same-target307 redirects, guards exact method/path/origin, owned body bytes, ContentLength/TransferEncoding/framing and operation microversion headers. Changed target/body/framing or Read/Close faults stop before second transport call, close inspected Body once, and remain sticky after a repairing native retry; valid body is replaced by owned bytes. Source/context/callback causes remain reachable.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_transport_test.go:TestImportVolumeBackupPhysicalRedirectCannotChangeBodyFramingOrScope`.
- API.ImportBackup and Connection.ImportVolumeBackup expose the same owned result while preserving their actual outer operation labels. Existing native API.Import remains independent: []byte record is base64 JSON, status201 only, and typed ID/Name DTO. New import retains literal string and admits original sub400 response to its full disconnected model; native Import is neither replaced nor classified by this Proxy review.
  Tests: `blockstorage/backup_import_service_test.go:TestBackupProxyImportUsesFullDisconnectedModelWhileNativeImportRetainsBase64AndTypedResult`, `blockstorage/backup_import_service_test.go:TestBackupProxyImportPreflightKeepsCorrectOuterOperationAndCauses`.

Python/Go differences:

- The pinned Backup.import_record overwrites caller url with backups/export_record and posts that value both as route and backup_url. Go deliberately repairs both to backups/import_record and the original literal locator; exact pinned Python wire parity is not claimed.
- Two explicit Go UTF-8 strings replace Python dynamically typed service/url. Empty/control/Unicode strings are admitted, while null, numeric and container arguments and Python mutable Resource/session/constructor identities are outside this typed contract. No automatic native bytes/base64 adapter is added.
- Source constructs cls() disconnected. Go returns independent nullable24 Value plus untyped BackupID, actual raw Backup and separately owned Discovery/Applied proof rather than the same mutable Resource; location is null without CurrentLocation, scope lookup or ID seed.
- Selected nonempty microversion is forwarded literally. Current upstream Keystoneauth Session normalization of prefixes/padding/tuple/latest and complete Adapter/discovery cache, link, endpoint, envelope precedence, legacy-header or deployment-specific HTTP behavior are not replicated. Upstream references are not evidence of running the exact installed dependency.
- The discovery parser maps utility-stage maximum-before-optional-minimum and tuple order/cap. Finite body-only candidate/shapes/status policy, Unicode16 decimal table and arbitrary-precision integer strings have explicit Go boundaries; CPython runtime digit limits and dependency endpoint-data exception order are not claimed. Accepted malformed discovery is terminal with actual proof, unlike paths that catch Keystoneauth DiscoveryFailure and return None.
- Known-only descriptor mapping retains raw wire members independently. Exact JSON number/truthiness, first insertion position for duplicate alias consumption, UTF-8/surrogate/depth/backend boundaries and dictionary identity differ from Python selected JSON/Requests runtime. Ordinary malformed UTF-8 JSON tolerance is bounded; valid wrong shape or descriptor failures remain errors with consumed ID and proof.
- Owned exchange admits finite HTTP100..399 and preserves native original-policy >=400 errors. Accepted bytes/header/status and Read/Close/source/context causes are independently observed without wait/rollback/replay. Native rejected POST IO discard remains a bounded limitation; only rejected discovery404/405 explicitly observes Read/Close for safe fallback.
- Fixed source/target/body/framing and microversion ownership constrain mutable native retry/redirect hooks while live authentication and bounded caller retry remain. This review covers only the actual pinned v3 Proxy.import_backup declaration; native/v2/Resource/inherited declarations and the full SDK remain independent. Source bindings and offline HTTP tests are not Python SDK execution or authenticated OpenStack integration.

### `python:block_storage/v3/reserve_volume`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage/v3/volumes.API.ReserveVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk.Connection.ReserveVolume`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage.ReserveVolume`

- ReserveVolume sends exactly {"os-reserve":null}. Use one explicit safe nonempty UTF-8 volume ID and the exact pinned null action through queryless POST volumes/{ID}/action. Unicode literal IDs are preserved, and VolumeDetail never inserts detail into the route. There is no UUID-only restriction, lookup, Resource conversion, status enum, options/defaults, builder, wait, refresh or rollback.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsPostSourceNullBodiesAndAcceptOpaqueHTTPReplies`, `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsRejectInvalidIDAndSourceBeforeAnyHTTP`.
- Direct calls capture the Cinder service and validate context/role/provider/endpoint/resource/header before ID admission. Connection checks context/provider/ID before selecting only cached Cinder v3, without CurrentLocation or recorded scope consumption. Getter errors retain the actual outer operation and custom cancellation causes before any request or fabricated proof.
  Tests: `connection_volume_state_actions_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeStateActionsUseOnlyCachedCinderAndIgnoreLocation`, `connection_volume_state_actions_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeStateActionsPreflightNeverSelectsCinder`, `connection_volume_state_actions_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeStateActionsGetterErrorsKeepOperationAndCancellation`.
- Selected nonempty microversion wins literally without discovery/cap. Otherwise operation-owned finite negotiation uses the Volume3.71 ceiling and server maximum before optional minimum, with tuple length/order retained; minimum above ceiling omits version and missing maximum ignores unused minimum. Original client/cache stays unchanged, each call owns its selected policy, and independent Backup import retains3.64 on the same server advertisement.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsSelectedVersionKeepsOwnedHeadersAndLiveNativeAuthentication`, `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsNegotiateFiniteVolumeCapWithoutChangingBackupCap`, `connection_volume_state_actions_test.go:TestConnectionVolumeStateActionsOwnDiscoveryPerCallAndKeepStageProof`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/backup_import_version_test.go:TestBackupImportVersionIndependentStringAndHeaderCases`, `internal/cloudsnapshot/backup_import_version_test.go:TestBackupImportVersionIndependentMaximumMinimumCases`.
- Finite discovery preserves captured encoded reverse-proxy prefix, admits200/300 supported body forms and first usable v3, and keeps admitted pages separate from action Applied. Only clean concrete native404/405 permits unavailable fallback. Accepted malformed/null/invalid-UTF8 or other discovery failures stop before action, with current ResponseError proof; later native rejection cannot borrow an earlier admitted page as current error proof.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsDiscoveryFailuresRetainCurrentStageAndNeverBorrowProof`.
- Action replies are opaque finite HTTP100..399 acknowledgements including empty, malformed and invalid-UTF8 bytes. Applied owns actual body/header/status, independently of separate calls or error proof. Completed means accepted Read/Close/source validation succeeded, never a server status postcondition. Original-policy>=400 and expanded native OkCodes retain native rejected evidence without Applied or Completed. Existing native Reserve/Unreserve/BeginDetaching keep their {} body and narrower status policy; all four new service methods and nil API preserve actual labels.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionAdmissionUsesOriginalSub400PolicyAndKeepsNativeRejections`, `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionServiceMethodsPreserveNativeBodiesCodesAndNilAPILabels`.
- Accepted Read/Close, captured-source drift and custom cancellation retain actual Applied and every joined cause, with Completed=false and an independently owned ResponseError. Body closes once and accepted IO errors invoke no caller retry or SDK replay; proof storage never aliases the caller response or another result.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_actions_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsAcceptedFaultsKeepActualProofAndAllCauses`, `blockstorage/volume_state_action_transport_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionsAcceptedFaultsKeepAppliedAndEveryCauseWithoutCompletion`.
- Live native401 reauthentication and bounded503 caller retry retain exact owned null body, captured ordinary headers, selected source and operation version. Body/decoder/retention changes, version conflicts or source drift stop before resend while original/callback/custom cancellation causes remain reachable.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_action_transport_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionLiveReauthAndBoundedRetryKeepLiteralNullBody`, `blockstorage/volume_state_action_transport_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionRetryMutationStopsBodyVersionAndSourceBeforeResend`.
- Every physical POST including same-target307 redirects verifies exact method/path/origin, body bytes/framing and modern/legacy version policy. Changed body/framing/header/target stops before second transport attempt. Physical body Read/Close and header faults remain sticky after caller repair, close inspected body once and preserve actual causes.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_state_action_transport_test.go:TestVolumeStateActionPhysicalRedirectMutationRemainsStickyAfterRepair`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go takes safe literal UTF-8 ID only. Source accepts mutable Volume/VolumeDetail/Resource/dict/Munch inputs through _get_resource with constructor/to_dict/descriptor/computed location side effects and Python urljoin coercion. Go skips those identities/side effects and name lookup deliberately; this does not classify separate Resource declarations.
- Source Proxy and Resource methods return None without translating response or updating local fields. Go adds independently owned Microversion, Discovery, Applied and Completed acknowledgement with literal input VolumeID. No semantic server-state confirmation, wait, follow-up GET or rollback is added.
- Volume uses class ceiling3.71 while Backup import keeps3.64 through the shared Cinder policy. Selected nonempty version is forwarded literally; Keystoneauth Session canonicalization, dynamic nonstring defaults, full Adapter cache/link/envelope/header discovery and deployment HTTP behavior are outside this owned Go mapping.
- Finite same-origin body-only first usable v3 discovery, typed advertisement shapes/status and clean concrete404/405 fallback are explicit mapping policy. Accepted malformed discovery is terminal with current proof rather than reproducing every caught Keystoneauth DiscoveryFailure/None path or endpoint-data exception order.
- Utility tuple selection retains maximum-before-optional-minimum, length/order and missing bounds. Shared arbitrary-precision Unicode16 decimal/underscore string parsing and latest handling are bounded Go behavior; exact installed CPython digit limits, nonstring input normalization and pinned dependency runtime are not claimed.
- Owned finite HTTP100..399 policy preserves opaque action bytes and admitted IO/source/context proof separately from native>=400 rejection. Native discarded rejected POST body Read/Close faults remain a documented limitation; mutable OkCodes cannot widen SDK acknowledgement. No response model conversion is introduced.
- Fixed source/method/route/body/framing and operation microversion ownership constrain mutable native retry/redirect hooks while live authentication and bounded caller retry remain. Native Reserve/Unreserve/BeginDetaching keep {} and narrower success codes; new null action APIs remain separate.
- Only the actual pinned v3 Proxy declaration is mapped here. Native/v2/Resource/inherited targets and direct bootable/readonly setters remain independent. Source hashes, static review, Go compile and offline HTTP/race tests do not establish authenticated OpenStack or Python SDK runtime parity or full SDK completion.

Live auth/reauth/bounded retry keep original body/version/admission; retry/physical307 body/framing/URL/version changes are blocked and native rejection cannot fabricate Applied. Service native SetImageMetadata nil=>null/200-only/error-only remains independent.
  Tests: `blockstorage/volume_image_metadata_transport_test.go:TestVolumeImageMetadataAcceptedFailuresRetainOnlyCurrentPhaseProofAndJoinedCauses`, `blockstorage/volume_image_metadata_transport_test.go:TestVolumeImageMetadataNativeRetriesAndPhysicalMutationGuardsKeepBatchPolicy`, `blockstorage/volume_image_metadata_test.go:TestVolumeImageMetadataServiceFacadePreservesNativeSetAndPublicPreflight`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python **metadata supplies a kwargs dictionary, including empty {}. Go string/raw/full factories replace request policy and Value merges; this is request assembly, not a claim that Cinder atomically replaces its stored image metadata.
- Go raw metadata permits owned ordinary JSON null/bool/numbers/containers without float preconversion, but rejects invalid UTF-8/incomplete JSON/nil raw members and excludes arbitrary Python objects/datetime/UUID/custom serializer behavior. Map member order and numeric/string/escape serialization are Go JSON representation boundaries, not Python mutable kwargs identity.
- Native API.SetImageMetadata remains independently map[string]string, nil metadata:null, HTTP200/error-only; ordinary MetadataIn childroutes are unchanged.
- Go safe explicit current volume ID and owned option/result values replace Source supplied Resource/dict/Munch/None/dynamic inherited ID and constructor/descriptor/location/session side effects. No name finder, CurrentLocation, refresh, state wait or model conversion is introduced.
- The Source helper returns None after response status<400. Go finite100..399 admission exposes owned actual stage responses and Completed acknowledgement; this does not prove the final server/backend metadata state.
- Go finite operation-owned ordinary discovery/cap3.71 and literal nonempty selected version differ from complete Keystoneauth endpoint-data cache/canonicalization/exception ordering. Empty Go selection cannot represent every distinct Python default None/empty value. Original client selection is not rewritten.
- Fixed method/URL/serialized-body/framing/version and captured Source constrain native retry/redirect while live provider authentication is allowed. Native original rejected HTTP remains native; discarded rejected HTTP body IO is not claimed fully reconstructed.
- Only the actual pinned v3 Proxy target is classified as go_mapping. Native/v2/Resource/MetadataMixin/ordinary MetadataIn and shared discovery dependencies are not promoted. Source/static comparison and Root offline Go HTTP/example checks do not imply Python/dependency/authenticated OpenStack execution or full SDK completion.

- The Source helper returns None after response status<400. Go finite100..399 admission exposes owned actual stage responses and Completed acknowledgement; this does not prove the final server/backend metadata state.

- Fixed method/URL/serialized-body/framing/version and captured Source constrain native retry/redirect while live provider authentication is allowed. Native original rejected HTTP remains native; discarded rejected HTTP body IO is not claimed fully reconstructed.

Direct Source/ID/image admission precedes originals; Connection prepares originals once before image/ID/cached Cinder selection and consumes no CurrentLocation.

Its selected value can be any JSON shape including null, nonobject and object without image_id; interior whitespace/numeric/escape literals are preserved and the last outer duplicate wins. - Discovery, Applied, selected Upload and response-error snapshots own independent bytes/headers. Accepted Read/Close/source/custom-cancellation failures keep the current actual phase and every cause, retain earlier completed discovery independently, close once and leave Upload nil/Completed false. Completed reports successful helper response handling without a Glance lookup, image wait, refresh or rollback.

Retry body/version/source mutations and physical redirected body/header/target/framing mutations block resend; original rejected HTTP retains native status/body/header with Applied nil and no borrowed discovery proof. The service facade keeps native UploadImage omitempty/202-only/typed VolumeImage behavior independently unchanged.

Python/Go differences:

- Go safe explicit current volume ID and UTF-8 literal image name replace Source supplied Resource/dict/Munch/None/dynamic inherited ID and constructor/descriptor/location/cache/session side effects. This helper adds no name finder, CurrentLocation, Volume model refresh, Glance lookup, image-byte upload, wait or rollback.
- Source annotations do not constrain runtime arguments: its own extended Proxy mock test forwards protected="5", and Python None/dynamic force can be forwarded literally. Go uses nullable typed strings/bools; Force nil means defaultfalse, optional nil means omission, and explicit empty/false remains present. Arbitrary Python runtime objects/custom serializers are outside this typed mapping.
- Source performs the3.1 requirement through endpoint-data utilities, then ordinary action selection through the Resource. Go captures one Source and uses finite operation-owned discovery/shared3.71 selection; this does not reproduce full Keystoneauth endpoint cache/getter count/canonicalization/exception order. Nonempty selected text is preserved; Go empty selection cannot represent every distinction between Python None and explicit empty default.
- The fixed3.1 gate compares3.1 with both advertised bounds and selected same-major minimum, not selected against the server maximum. A selected3.90 can pass advertised3.0..3.50 and is sent literally; global latest fails the same-major gate whereas3.latest passes. Dependency Session normalization/header behavior remains a separate declared boundary.
- The Source cast(dict, ...) is only an annotation cast and returns the exact selected JSON value; image_id and object shape are not required. Go returns owned literal RawMessage, preserving interior numeric/escape/whitespace spelling rather than Python mutable parsed objects/float rounding. Outer duplicate envelope keys follow ordinary last-key JSON decoding; successful null is nonnil literalnull, unlike error nil Upload.
- Go validates accepted UTF-8 JSON and an exact object envelope before exposing Upload, and exposes separate Discovery/Applied/Upload/Completed evidence. Accepted decode/IO/source/context faults keep actual completed HTTP proof without replay. These error/evidence choices do not promise identical Python exception taxonomy or final image/backend availability.
- Source raise_from_response admits status<400; Go uses finite100..399 and constrains native retry/redirect through fixed physical method/URL/body/framing/version and captured Source while permitting live provider auth. Original rejected HTTP remains native; discarded native rejected-body IO is not claimed fully reconstructed.
- Only the actual pinned v3 Proxy upload_volume_to_image target is classified as go_mapping. Native UploadImage omitempty/202-only/typed VolumeImage, Glance image.Service.UploadImage, v2/Resource/shared discovery dependencies are not promoted. Static Source/dependency comparison and Root offline Go HTTP/example checks do not imply Python/dependency/authenticated OpenStack runtime or full SDK completion.

### `python:block_storage/v3/create_image`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk.Connection.CreateVolumeImageRecord`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.ExistingImageRecord`

- CreateVolumeImageRecord fills a falsey disk format from the captured cloud image_format (default qcow2, omitted when configured null), a falsey container format with bare and force from allow_duplicates, then sends one upload-to-image volume action through the existing Cinder upload engine.
  Tests: `connection_volume_image_create_test.go:TestConnectionCreateVolumeImageRecordDefaultsUploadAndExistingImage`.
- The upload result's image_id becomes Image.existing(id=...) through image.Service.ExistingImageRecord: a synchronized id-only record with the 65 declared fields and Connection location and no Glance request.
  Tests: `connection_volume_image_create_test.go:TestConnectionCreateVolumeImageRecordDefaultsUploadAndExistingImage`.
- A non-object result, missing, null, non-string or blank image_id returns the upload evidence with an invalid-option error under the Cloud operation; a non-string configured format and invalid volume IDs fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `connection_volume_image_create_test.go:TestConnectionCreateVolumeImageRecordFailuresKeepUploadEvidence`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python accepts a Volume Resource or ID and returns an Image Resource; Go takes a literal VolumeID and returns VolumeImageCreateResult separating the Cinder upload evidence from the ImageRecord.
- The pinned Proxy accepts wait and timeout but never reads them, so Go omits them. Python sends any configured image_format value; Go requires a string or null configuration and a nonblank string image_id.

## Usage examples

Examples use the surrounding guide's service object and context unless they declare a complete program. Python examples describe the pinned comparison API.

```python
volume = conn.block_storage.find_volume("data", ignore_missing=False)
for volume in conn.block_storage.volumes(status="available"):
    print(volume.id, volume.size)
```

```go
service, err := conn.BlockStorage(ctx)
if err != nil { return err }
volume, err := service.Volumes.Find(ctx, resource.Name("data"))
if err != nil { return err }
fmt.Println(volume.ID, volume.Size)

for volume, err := range service.Volumes.List(ctx,
    resource.WithStatus("available"), resource.WithPageSize(100)) {
    if err != nil { return err }
    fmt.Println(volume.ID, volume.Size)
}
```

```go
ready, err := service.Volumes.Wait(ctx, resource.ID(volume.ID), "available",
    resource.WithTimeout(5*time.Minute))
if err != nil { return err }
fmt.Println(ready.Status)

if err := service.Volumes.Delete(ctx, resource.ID(volume.ID)); err != nil {
    return err
}
```
